Dance\",\"videoPrompt\":\"Animate this subject dancing energetically and confidently for exactly 6 seconds in smooth 30fps, funky disco choreography matching this precise high-energy sequence: 0.0–1.0s: Begin in the starting pose from the reference image, then burst into motion with dramatic arm point like Saturday Night Fever while raising one leg high bent at the knee, hips swaying with attitude. 1.0–2.2s: Transition to fast rhythmic hip shakes side-to-side, arms performing big expressive circular disco swings, head bobbing in sync 2.2–3.5s: Execute a smooth 360° clockwise spin, arms raising in a V-shape then crossing body, ending with a powerful forward hip thrust and sharp pose accent. 3.5–4.8s: Fast dynamic footwork with deep knee bends and side-to-side steps, arms alternating stylish pumps and rolls, full body showing playful confident grin 4.8–6.0s: Build to climax with double arms raised overhead while doing a final big hip shake, then drop into a confident wide stance and hold a strong proud victory pose with head tilted back for the last 0.3–0.5 seconds. Make sure the character remains fully dressed with proper pants throughout the entire video.\",\"postID\":\"7c1a550f-6a03-4d6b-8eea-83bbd5911dc9\",\"description\":\"Makes you dance funky disco with spins, hip shakes, and victory pose.\"},{\"title\":\"Chibi\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Transform the subjects into a cute anime chibi characters rendered as a clean 3D model, while preserving the subject’s original race, ethnicity, skin tone, and recognizable facial likeness, including hairstyle, hair color, and clothing colors. If the input image does not clearly show clothing, generate appropriate casual clothing that naturally fits the character so the subject is always clothed. The character must remain clearly the same person with the same skin tone and ethnic appearance as the original input, translated into a chibi form. The character should have classic chibi proportions: a very large head, small torso, simplified cute facial features, and large expressive anime-style eyes. Translate the subject’s facial features into a chibi style while keeping their recognizable likeness. Frame the character from the torso up, with the camera slightly closer and cropped in, so the upper body and head fill most of the frame. The character should be looking directly into the camera, with an energetic and expressive anime-style pose. Use a slightly dynamic camera angle, such as a subtle tilt or off-axis perspective, similar to modern anime promotional artwork. The camera can be slightly lower than the character’s face, creating a playful upward angle that makes the character feel lively and engaging. Render the character as a high-quality glossy 3D anime-style model, similar to a polished Blender render or premium collectible figure. The materials should be smooth, reflective, and toy-like, with strong highlights, soft reflections, and shiny surfaces. The overall look should clearly resemble a 3D rendered character, not a flat 2D illustration. Use bright anime-style shading with vibrant but polished colors, giving the character a premium anime aesthetic. Place the character in front of a colorful gradient background. Behind the character, include a small number of large, translucent decorative shapes such as bubbles, stars, or rounded forms. These shapes should appear semi-transparent, glossy, and subtle, similar to clear acrylic or glass, softly floating in the background without drawing attention away from the character. The background elements should remain light, minimal, and softly visible, acting as stylish accents rather than prominent objects. The final image should look like a dynamic, glossy 3D chibi character render, with polished materials, vibrant colors, and a premium collectible aesthetic, while clearly maintaining the subject’s original race, skin tone, and recognizable identity.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"54009e77-72a1-4189-a650-731631a55838\",\"description\":\"Transforms you into an adorable, big-eyed 3D chibi-style cartoon avatar.\"},{\"postID\":\"94d86171-ef2e-40fa-9f4d-45457a4a47b8\",\"title\":\"Add Girlfriend\",\"prompt\":\"add a girlfriend\",\"description\":\"Adds a girlfriend to the scene.\"},{\"title\":\"Comic Book\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Transform the input image into the style of a classic 1960s–1970s American comic book illustration while preserving the subject’s recognizable identity, pose, clothing, and overall scene composition. Reinterpret the subject as if drawn for a vintage superhero comic panel: bold black ink outlines, simplified shapes, dynamic shading, and flat color blocks typical of old comic printing. The face should remain recognizable but slightly stylized with stronger jawline, clearer cheekbones, expressive eyes, and confident comic-book character energy. Clothing and environment should remain based on the original image but translated into comic-style textures and colors. Add subtle halftone printing dots and vintage comic coloring to emulate the look of a classic printed superhero comic page.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"7bf6566f-30b5-4bcf-bfd1-fb296fac5edb\",\"description\":\"Turns your photo into classic 1960s-1970s comic book style.\"},{\"title\":\"Product Photography\",\"description\":\"Places your product in real-life lifestyle scenes.\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Using the exact product in \u003cIMAGE_0\u003e, place it into a fresh, natural real-world lifestyle scene while keeping it 100% identical in every detail: design, colors, materials, textures, logos, branding and proportions unchanged. The new environment should be an engaging modern context such as a contemporary home, luxury interior, outdoor setting, urban lifestyle or premium everyday space — choose the most flattering one. Perfectly integrate with realistic lighting, shadows and reflections. Creatively control the mood and atmosphere to enhance the brand feel. High-end commercial product photography, photorealistic, maximum detail.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"imagePreviewURL\":\"https://imagine-public.x.ai/imagine-public/share-images/f67a9ec2-4bc2-400b-b584-fb65236abb5f.jpg\",\"postID\":\"f67a9ec2-4bc2-400b-b584-fb65236abb5f\"},{\"title\":\"Gigachad\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Black and white headshot up close head shot, low angle upward tilt, tight skin no strange creases perfectly taught across subjects face, dramatically pronounced and increased jaw size, update the subject’s facial structure and anatomy so they have roman carving level jawline, overly exaggerated pronounced jawline, and accentuated cheekbones, overly hyper masculine facial structure, exaggerated facial structure, dramatic shadows, increased jaw size and width. Young, good smooth skin, muscular updated build. Dark background. Exaggerated facial features and muscular build. Retain clothing and hair style. Subject should look have superhero level anatomy. Photo is low angle at a slight angle, up close to the subject face highlighting the subjects dramatically strong jawline. The subject has incredible smooth tight skin, dramatically intense jaw structure, almost animated looking in it's composition. Skin is updated to be flawless and tight against facial bone features.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"234653bd-8e5b-4f24-99da-f2c629d8ec51\",\"description\":\"Turns you into a super muscular Gigachad.\"},{\"postID\":\"5a1ee5d0-6a82-4a02-b5ec-e1558cc69981\",\"title\":\"Soda Pop\",\"prompt\":\"The subjects as if drinking with their right hand in a call me / shaka / hang ten hand gesture :call_me_hand: (thumb and pinky extended, other fingers curled, pinky toward the mouth like holding and sipping from a soda can), left hand on hip, in a dance-like way doing a tilting/pouring motion into their mouth with a slight head tilt back, and then does a dynamic vibey k-pop style dance. Camera motion adjusts to capture the full frame of the subject. Super active choreographed k-pop and hiphop style dance moves, camera lock, final cool landing position at the end.\",\"description\":\"Sips soda with a cool hand gesture then dances K-pop style.\"},{\"title\":\"Haze Portrait\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Transform the input photo into a cinematic chromatic haze portrait of the same person, preserving their identity, facial structure, skin tone, hairstyle, expression, and overall styling. Recompose the image as a subtle three-quarter side portrait, with the subject turned slightly away from camera and gazing off-frame. Create a soft double-exposure-like chromatic offset around the face and hair, with gentle red/orange glow on one side and cool cyan/blue glow on the other, producing a dreamy optical ghosting effect rather than a glitch effect. Keep the portrait clean, minimal, and editorial. Use soft diffused studio lighting, shallow depth of field, smooth skin rendering, and a calm high-end fashion photography feel. The image should feel atmospheric and futuristic, with luminous color bleed, soft motion haze, feathered edges, and a polished fine-art beauty portrait look. Background should be plain, bright, and unobtrusive, fading into soft white or cream. Preserve realism in the face while adding the chromatic haze treatment around contours and profile edges. No harsh glitch artifacts, no heavy distortion, no text, no busy background, no extra objects.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"9ab62ecc-a117-4ff1-8c0d-8890e54c25ff\",\"description\":\"Creates a dreamy glowing haze portrait.\"},{\"title\":\"Showcase\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Using the uploaded product image as reference, create a crisp photoreal product hero shot on a smooth, minimal gradient backdrop (subtle tonal shift, not colorful rainbow). Soft studio lighting with one large diffused key light and gentle fill; clean shadow under the product; faint highlight edge that matches its material. No props, no clutter. Product proportions and branding must remain exact and readable. Shot like high-end ecommerce photography, 70mm lens look, tack-sharp details, neutral color accuracy. Provide ample negative space for storefront layout.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"Very subtle cinematic pan to the right, keeping the object centered in the frame throughout. Steady, smooth, aesthetic.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"videoPreviewURL\":\"https://imagine-public.x.ai/imagine-public/share-videos/7b097052-8cfe-4b0f-8a33-c2a71e1aed25.mp4\",\"postID\":\"7b097052-8cfe-4b0f-8a33-c2a71e1aed25\",\"description\":\"Creates a clean studio product photo with soft lighting and a smooth pan.\"},{\"title\":\"Roman Empire\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Transform the person in the input image into a cinematic ancient Roman epic scene, as if they are a central character in a large-scale historical blockbuster film.Preserve the subject’s recognizable identity from the original image — including their facial structure, hairstyle, skin tone, age, and overall likeness — while adapting their appearance to fit the world of ancient Rome.Frame the scene as a dramatic cinematic composition, showing the subject in a three-quarter body view, allowing the armor, stance, and surrounding Roman environment to be clearly visible.Style the subject as a Roman warrior, general, or noble figure, wearing ornate epic Roman armor that feels sculpted and organic, as if it was forged specifically for them.The armor should appear cinematic, mythic, and heroic, inspired by ancient Roman design but elevated to the scale of a blockbuster historical epic.Typical armor characteristics may include: • sculpted metal surfaces that follow the contours of the body • engraved patterns inspired by Roman mythology • layered metal and leather elements integrated into the design • intricate ornamental details and symbolic engravings • subtle battle-worn textures and aged materials Do not include any cape or cloak. The armor should stand on its own without a red cape or flowing fabric.The armor should feel slightly organic and artistic rather than rigid, with elegant curves, sculptural shapes, and intricate detailing that make it appear legendary and powerful.Hair and grooming should remain recognizable from the original image while being adapted to fit the historical setting.The subject should have a serious, focused, and inspirational expression, conveying strength, resolve, and leadership. Their face should appear calm, determined, and heroic, as if they are preparing for battle or inspiring those around them.Place the subject in a grand Roman environment, such as: • a massive Roman battlefield at dawn• the steps of an ancient Roman temple• the Roman Colosseum in the background • a dramatic imperial cityscape with marble columns and statues. The environment should feel large, epic, and cinematic, with atmospheric depth, dust or smoke in the air, and distant figures or architecture reinforcing the scale.Lighting should feel dramatic and cinematic, similar to a modern historical epic film, with warm sunlight cutting through dust or clouds, creating strong highlights and deep shadows. Render the image with the visual style of a high-budget historical blockbuster, featuring: • cinematic color grading • dramatic lighting • detailed costume textures • atmospheric depth and scale.The final image should resemble a frame from an epic ancient Roman film, where the subject appears as a heroic character in a grand historical narrative.Preserve realism, natural anatomy, and believable materials so the result looks like a real cinematic scene rather than an illustration or costume portrait.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"9d8f6c8e-3507-4f38-a022-3c14f6fbbd91\",\"description\":\"Travel back in time to the age of great Roman Emperors.\"},{\"title\":\"Fire Horse\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Subject is riding on a beautiful vibrantly red giant porcelain horse, positioned in front of the great wall of china. Slightly grainy film texture. Low dramatic epic angle. Beautiful framing. High resolution, up close framing of subject upper body. Low light background. Subject has a neutral facial expression.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"Horse begins galloping, background becomes darker and golden/yellow fireworks go off in background.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"808ab93b-ec7f-4052-964b-5174c908db1d\",\"description\":\"Rides a giant red porcelain horse galloping by the Great Wall with fireworks.\"},{\"title\":\"Video Game\",\"imagePrompt\":\"A character rendered in authentic early-2000s PlayStation 2 / Nintendo 64 video game style, with very low-poly geometry, blocky limbs, and angular facial features. The character has simple, flat textures, visible texture stretching, and low-resolution diffuse maps, with no skin pores, no fine detail, and no realism.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"f51ad8c5-9dfa-4436-8c5a-9f0e95f78483\",\"description\":\"Turns life into a video game.\"},{\"title\":\"Ad Astra\",\"imagePrompt\":\"The subjects are suspended in space, wearing spacesuits. Entire image is black and white.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"They are gently floating through space, subtly happy and in awe.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"9068fa4b-4805-467c-b2d3-0cb503356052\",\"description\":\"Floats you through space in a black-and-white spacesuit.\"},{\"title\":\"Balloon Flight\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Turn the core characters from the image into inflated, glossy vinyl Balloons figure with huge, oversized eyes, gently floating and rising up into the sky. The balloons only have the overinflated, rounded upper body and arms. A thread holds the bottom of the balloon. Highly reflective smooth plastic surface, subtle seam lines, gentle pressure wrinkles, shiny toy-like finish, soft, inflated human face with slightly simplified features, cinematic studio lighting, centered portrait, ultra-detailed, polished inflatable character aesthetic， medium close-up shot.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"The printed eyes of balloons are static; the balloons float purely upward; the balloons themselves are completely rigid, devoid of any movement. The camera tracks the balloon statically, ensuring it remains centered within the center of the frame.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"3075a66c-40f6-47ed-b86a-6a649a7bdf0b\",\"description\":\"Turn yourself into a balloon and rise high into the sky.\"},{\"title\":\"Go Skiing\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Subjects skiing down a snowy mountain. Preserve outfit.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"Skiing in a zigzag carving motion.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"099aeb0e-6221-4a45-889c-f90b6626a627\",\"description\":\"Skiing down a snowy mountain carving turns.\"},{\"title\":\"Laser Fight\",\"imagePrompt\":\"the subjects each hold a blue, futuristic lightsaber sword in their hands\",\"videoPrompt\":\"the characters start fighting with the lightsaber in a controlled, elegant way. cinematic darkness emerges.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"c9614d47-b75d-40c2-b9cf-97394c099b9d\",\"description\":\"Fights with glowing blue lightsabers.\"},{\"postID\":\"468c842f-26c3-4f08-8d50-ee3e6b60b1b1\",\"title\":\"Future Family\",\"prompt\":\"Add their children looking like them and dressed similarly.\",\"description\":\"Adds kids who look like you.\"},{\"postID\":\"7be5f03e-0484-4a12-aad7-f14159820ffa\",\"title\":\"Club Beats\",\"prompt\":\"Create a short video animation starting from the uploaded static image of a person. The subject(s) begins without any accessories, facing forward with a neutral expression. Smoothly animate them reaching up and putting on large black visor-style sunglasses that cover their eyes completely, followed by sliding on bulky over-ear headphones or ear protectors. Once equipped, have them start nodding their head rhythmically to upbeat music, with subtle body sways, maintaining a cool, intense vibe like a video game character. Cinematic lighting illuminates the face of the subject and the camera dolly's in as the subject continues bopping their head, keeping the body movement realistic and fluid.\",\"description\":\"Puts on sunglasses and headphones then grooves to club beats.\"},{\"title\":\"Simulationship\",\"imagePrompt\":\"The subject is running in a field with a few flowers, ultra grainy and low saturation 90's film aesthetic retro, holding the hand of silhouette figure that has a vintage neon green grid pattern (almost like a simulation character), they are running through a grassy field on a sunny day, a few flowers in the field. Both subjects are joyous.\",\"videoPrompt\":\"Subjects continue holding hands playfully running through the grass, almost dream-like.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I2V\",\"postID\":\"117cd5d1-ba11-4e93-bd93-160c4f92babf\",\"description\":\"Runs hand-in-hand through a flower field with a glowing simulation figure.\"},{\"postID\":\"ad4741bf-8f64-43f8-bba4-9097b5b5a14d\",\"title\":\"Let it Snow\",\"prompt\":\"A powdery snowball thrown at the subject hitting them in their face, snowball bursting into powdery snow on impact. Funny and harmless. The character looks slightly upset looking in the direction from where the snowball came and calling toward that direction at someone beyond the camera.\",\"description\":\"Gets hit in the face by a snowball.\"},{\"postID\":\"55c2e33d-94f1-49e2-bee4-0984cb28e7be\",\"title\":\"Bear Buddy\",\"prompt\":\"A giant alive and real polar bear walks up to the subject sitting next to the subject, and the subject pats the bear as the bear softly snuggles the subject. One continuous shot, no cuts.\",\"description\":\"Cuddles with a giant friendly polar bear.\"},{\"postID\":\"3c36fe5c-01ce-4a74-8102-d8eaa8443e66\",\"title\":\"Cuddle a Squirrel\",\"prompt\":\"A little squirrel runs up the shoulder of the subject, and they touch noses softly and sweetly.\",\"description\":\"A squirrel climbs your shoulder for a nose touch.\"},{\"postID\":\"7a668278-27c6-497e-96ea-552740c4ab15\",\"title\":\"Helicopter Escape\",\"prompt\":\"A rescue rope drops from above as the subject grabs it and vanishes skyward. Action-packed, cinematic getaway\",\"description\":\"Grabs a rope and escapes by helicopter.\"},{\"title\":\"Sunny Morning\",\"imagePrompt\":\"Warm, nostalgic 35mm film still aesthetic with a soft 1970s/early-80s pastoral-cinema vibe. Golden-hour backlight flooding the frame: rich amber/orange highlights, warm skin tones, and honey-colored grasses. Gentle film halation (soft glowing bloom around bright areas and sunlit edges), slightly lifted blacks with a mild matte fade, and smooth highlight roll-off (no harsh clipping). Medium-wide composition with the subject placed left-of-frame and lots of negative space to the right; a calm, observational, “quiet moment” tone. Shallow-to-moderate depth of field: foreground grasses softly blur into the lens, subject readable but not razor sharp, background trees softened. Naturalistic contrast, slightly low micro-contrast, creamy texture. Subtle film grain throughout (fine, organic grain), mild softness and lens character (gentle vignetting, slight flare), and a restrained color palette that leans warm gold + muted greens. Overall look: dreamy, intimate, sun-drenched, painterly realism like a still from an art-house coming-of-age film.\",\"templateType\":\"I2I\",\"postID\":\"7ad8ee24-d5d5-4688-84bb-b221cddc5dd7\",\"description\":\"Warm golden-hour morning scene with film all of my stored memories and any context you've learned about me from past conversations. Preserve my words verbatim where possible, especially for instructions and preferences.\\n\\n## Categories (output in this order):\\n\\n1. **Instructions**: Rules I've explicitly asked you to follow going forward — tone, format, style, \\\"always do X\\\", \\\"never do Y\\\", and corrections to your behavior. Only include rules from stored memories, not from conversations.\\n\\n2. **Identity**: Name, age, location, education, family, relationships, languages, and personal interests.\\n\\n3. **Career**: Current and past roles, companies, and general skill areas.\\n\\n4. **Projects**: Projects I meaningfully built or committed to. Ideally ONE entry per project. Include what it does, current status, and any key decisions. Use the project name or a short descriptor as the first words of the entry.\\n\\n5. **Preferences**: Opinions, tastes, and working-style preferences that apply broadly.\\n\\n## Format:\\n\\nUse section headers for each category. Within each category, list one entry per line, sorted by oldest date first. Format each line as:\\n\\n[YYYY-MM-DD] - Entry content here.\\n\\nIf no date is known, use [unknown] instead.\\n\\n## Output:\\n- Wrap the entire export in a single code block for easy copying.\\n- After the code block, state whether this is the complete set or if more remain.\",\"enable_memory_import\":true,\"query_bar_accessibility\":{\"enabled\":true,\"text\":\"Agent (Beta)\",\"dynamic_pills\":[{\"icon\":\"photo-stack\",\"prompts\":[{\"label\":\"Create Worlds\",\"prompt\":\"First, generate 6 world concepts and their infographics. Then show a scene from each world.\"},{\"label\":\"Character Sheets\",\"prompt\":\"First, create 6 character sheets in landscape. Then edit each sheet to get a portrait model of each character.\"}]},{\"icon\":\"film-roll\",\"prompts\":[{\"label\":\"Short Film\",\"prompt\":\"Give me 3 quick ideas for a 1 minute film. Then, generate an image of the main character in each film. Then use that image to create a movie poster for each film.\"},{\"label\":\"Manga One-Shot\",\"prompt\":\"Give me 3 quick ideas for a short manga one-shot. For each, generate the main character, then create a sequence of manga panels for a key scene.\"}]},{\"icon\":\"camera\",\"prompts\":[{\"label\":\"UGC Product Stories\",\"prompt\":\"Generate 3 distinct products and 3 diverse realistic everyday models. First, create reference images of each product and natural lifestyle portraits for each model. Then develop 3 UGC concept stories mixing the products and models. For each story, generate candid user-style photos or keyframes of the model using the product, then use those images to create complete social media packs (carousels, Stories, Reels covers, and posts).\"},{\"label\":\"UGC Scenes\",\"prompt\":\"Generate 3 authentic UGC-style product-in-use scene concepts showing everyday people with the product. For each, first create candid, natural-looking user photos from different angles, then use the strongest images to build complete ready-to-post Instagram/TikTok carousels, Reels covers, and story templates\"}]},{\"icon\":\"layout\",\"prompts\":[{\"label\":\"Brand Identity\",\"prompt\":\"Create 2 distinct brand identity directions for a product or company. For each direction, first generate core visual assets as high-quality images (logo variations, color palette, typography samples), then use those generated images to produce realistic application mockups on business cards, website headers, packaging, and marketing materials.\"},{\"label\":\"Cover Concepts\",\"prompt\":\"Create 4 different cover concepts for a report, ebook, whitepaper, or book. Privacy Preference CenterWhen you visit any website, it may store or retrieve information on your browser, mostly in the form of cookies. This information might be about you, your preferences, or your device, and is mostly used to make the site work as you expect. The information does not usually identify you directly, but it can give you a more personalized web experience. Because we respect your right to privacy, you can choose not to allow some types of cookies. Click on the different category headings to learn more and change our default settings. Blocking some types of cookies may impact your experience of the site and the services we are able to offer.
            More informationAllow All Manage Consent PreferencesStrictly Necessary CookiesAlways ActiveThese cookies are necessary for the website to function and cannot be switched off in our systems. They are usually only set in response to actions made by you which amount to a request for services, such as setting your privacy preferences, logging in or filling in forms. You can set your browser to block or alert you about these cookies, but some parts of the site will not then work. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable information.Targeting Cookies  Targeting Cookies These cookies may be set through our site by our advertising partners. They may be used by those companies to build a profile of your interests and show you relevant adverts on other sites. They do not store directly personal information, but are based on uniquely identifying your browser and internet device. If you do not allow these cookies, you will experience less targeted advertising.Performance Cookies  Performance Cookies These cookies allow us to count visits and traffic sources so we can measure and improve the performance of our site. They help us to know which pages are the most and least popular and see how visitors move around the site. All information these cookies collect is aggregated and therefore anonymous. If you do not allow these cookies we will not know when you have visited our site, and will not be able to monitor its performance.Back ButtonCookie List Search IconFilter IconClear checkbox label labelApply CancelConsent Leg.Interest checkbox label label checkbox label label checkbox label labelReject All Confirm My Choices
